- `ClassDeclaration = { Modifier } "class" Name [ GenericParameters ] [ BaseList ] Block .` — 引用类型，无默认内存分配方式，支持单继承、多接口实现、多态和方法重写；类型体直接包含字段、模块与扩展块（见 [3.8.1](#381-类)）
- `StructureDeclaration = { Modifier } "structure" Name [ GenericParameters ] Block .` — 值类型，无默认内存分配方式；类型体直接包含字段、模块与扩展块（见 [3.8.2](#382-结构体)）
- `BaseList = ":" ( Identifier | MonomorphizationExpression ) { "," ( Identifier | MonomorphizationExpression ) } .` — 被继承或被实现目标的列表（见 [3.8.8](#388-继承列表)）

有关表达式：

- `IsExpression = Expression "is" IsTarget . …` — 判定形态的表达式（见 [4.8](#48-is-表达式)）
- `ValueExpression = Identifier …` — 表示值的表达式（见 [4.1](#41-值表达式)）
- `FunctionCallExpression = Expression "(" Arguments ")" .` — 调用函数的表达式（见 [4.5](#45-函数调用表达式)）
- `MemberConstructionExpression = ConstructedTypeName "." Name "(" Expression ")" .` — 以成员构造宿主实例的表达式（见 [4.7](#47-成员构造表达式)）
- `ParenthesisExpression = "(" Expression ")" .` — 改变求值优先级的表达式（见 [4.2](#42-括号表达式)）
- `ConstructionExpression = ConstructedTypeName "(" Arguments ")" . …` — 构造实例的表达式（见 [4.6](#46-构造表达式)）
- `TypeConversionExpression = Expression "as" Type .` — 以显式转换将值转换为目标类型的表达式（见 [4.3](#43-类型转换表达式)）
- `Expression = ValueExpression …` — 一类带有返回值的语法形式，用于计算和求值（见 [4](#4-表达式)）
- `VerbatimExpression = "@" "(" Expression ")" .` — 禁止优化的表达式（见 [14.3.1](#1431-不优化标记)）
- `MemberAccessExpression = ( Identifier | Expression ) "." Name .` — 获取标识符、类型或实例的成员的表达式（见 [4.4.1](#441-成员访问表达式)）
- `PointerMemberAccessExpression = ( Identifier | Expression ) "->" Name .` — 获取标识符或实例所承载指针的目标实例的成员的表达式（见 [4.4.2](#442-指针成员访问表达式)）
- `IndexAccessExpression = ( Identifier | Expression ) "[" Expression "]" .` — 通过索引获取标识符或实例的内容的表达式（见 [4.4.3](#443-索引访问表达式)）
- `AccessExpression = MemberAccessExpression …` — 描述对标识符或实例进行访问的表达式（见 [4.4](#44-访问表达式)）
- `NotEqualExpression = Expression "!=" Expression .` — 对判断两个值是否不等的表达式（见 [4.9.22](#4922-不等表达式)）
- `MultiplyExpression = Expression "*" Expression .` — 对两个值进行求积的表达式（见 [4.9.8](#498-乘法表达式)）
- `MinusExpression = Expression "-" Expression .` — 对两个值进行求差的表达式（见 [4.9.12](#4912-减法表达式)）
- `AddExpression = Expression "+" Expression .` — 对两个值进行求和的表达式（见 [4.9.11](#4911-加法表达式)）
- `NegateExpression = "-" Expression .` — 对操作数取算术负值的表达式（见 [4.9.3](#493-取负表达式)）
- `GreaterEqualExpression = Expression ">=" Expression .` — 对判断两个值是否大于等于的表达式（见 [4.9.20](#4920-大于等于表达式)）
- `GreaterExpression = Expression ">" Expression .` — 对判断两个值是否大于的表达式（见 [4.9.18](#4918-大于表达式)）
- `LessEqualExpression = Expression "<=" Expression .` — 对判断两个值是否小于等于的表达式（见 [4.9.19](#4919-小于等于表达式)）
- `LessExpression = Expression "<" Expression .` — 对判断两个值是否小于的表达式（见 [4.9.17](#4917-小于表达式)）
- `ReferenceExpression = "&" Expression .` — 获取实例地址的表达式（见 [4.9.6](#496-引用表达式)）
- `BitwiseAndExpression = Expression "&" Expression .` — 对两个值进行按位 AND 操作的表达式（见 [4.9.23](#4923-按位与表达式)）
- `BitwiseNotExpression = "^" Expression .` — 对一个值进行按位 NOT 操作的表达式（见 [4.9.5](#495-按位取反表达式)）
- `BitwiseXorExpression = Expression "^" Expression .` — 对两个值进行按位 XOR 操作的表达式（见 [4.9.24](#4924-按位异或表达式)）
- `BitwiseOrExpression = Expression "|" Expression .` — 对两个值进行按位 OR 操作的表达式（见 [4.9.25](#4925-按位或表达式)）
- `ConditionalExpression = Expression "?" Expression ":" Expression .` — 根据条件在两个值之间选取的表达式（见 [4.9.29](#4929-条件表达式)）
- `RemainderExpression = Expression "%" Expression .` — 对两个值进行求余的表达式（见 [4.9.10](#4910-求余表达式)）
- `EqualExpression = Expression "==" Expression .` — 对判断两个值是否相等的表达式（见 [4.9.21](#4921-相等表达式)）
- `NullCoalescingExpression = Expression "??" CoalescingAlternative . …` — 以 `??` 对左操作数进行决策，并在决策为 `Alternative` 时执行右侧续体的表达式（见 [4.9.28](#4928-空值合并表达式)）
- `DereferenceExpression = "*" Expression .` — 获取指针所指向实例的表达式（见 [4.9.7](#497-解引用表达式)）
- `OperatorDefinition = { Modifier } "operator" OperatorHead [ "=" Expression | Block | AccessorMembers ] . …` — 为运算符提供运算行为的标识符种类（见 [4.9](#49-运算符)）
- `OperatorHead = OperatorShorthand | OperatorNotation [ ":" Type ] . …` — 运算符头部（见 [4.9](#49-运算符)）
- `OperatorShorthand = OperatorSymbol "(" [ Parameters ] ")" [ ":" Type ] . …` — 运算符简写（见 [4.9](#49-运算符)）
- 表达式结合次序的规范（见 [4.9.1](#491-运算符优先级)）
- 为运算符提供符合自定义类型的运算行为的机制（见 [4.9.2](#492-运算符重载)）
- `LogicalRightShiftExpression = Expression ">>" Expression .` — 对两个值进行逻辑右移操作的表达式（见 [4.9.14](#4914-逻辑右移表达式)）
- `LogicalAndExpression = Expression "&&" Expression .` — 对两个布尔值进行 AND 操作的表达式（见 [4.9.26](#4926-逻辑和表达式)）
- `LogicalLeftShiftExpression = Expression "<<" Expression .` — 对两个值进行逻辑左移操作的表达式（见 [4.9.13](#4913-逻辑左移表达式)）
- `CircularLeftShiftExpression = Expression "<<<" Expression .` — 对两个值进行回环左移操作的表达式（见 [4.9.15](#4915-回环左移表达式)）
- `CircularRightShiftExpression = Expression ">>>" Expression .` — 对两个值进行回环右移操作的表达式（见 [4.9.16](#4916-回环右移表达式)）
- `LogicalOrExpression = Expression "||" Expression .` — 对两个布尔值进行 OR 操作的表达式（见 [4.9.27](#4927-逻辑或表达式)）
- `LogicalNotExpression = "!" Expression .` — 对一个布尔值进行取反的表达式（见 [4.9.4](#494-逻辑非表达式)）
- `DivideExpression = Expression "/" Expression .` — 对两个值进行求商的表达式（见 [4.9.9](#499-除法表达式)）

有关语句：

- `DisposeStatement = "dispose" ( Identifier | PointerMemberAccessExpression ) .` — 销毁堆存储的语句（见 [5.9](#59-dispose-语句)）
- `Block = "{" { Statement | Block } "}" .` — 包含语句序列的作用域（见 [5.1](#51-代码块)）
- `VerbatimBlock = "@" Block .` — 禁止优化的代码块（见 [14.3.1](#1431-不优化标记)）
- `AssemblyStatement = "assembly" [ "with" AssemblyClobbers ] AssemblyBlock . …` — 在函数体中嵌入目标架构汇编文本（见 [5.2](#52-内联汇编)）
- `CompileTimeIf = "#if" Expression CompileTimeBranch [ "else" CompileTimeBranch ] . …` — 编译期条件选择（见 [11.6](#116-条件编译)）
- `LocalDefinition = { Modifier } "local" [ "@" ] LocalItem { "," LocalItem } . …` — 在当前作用域内取得实例存储的标识符种类（见 [5.3](#53-local)）
- `LocalItem = Name [ ":" Type ] [ "=" Expression ] . …` — 局部变量定义项（见 [5.3](#53-local)）
- `HeapDefinition = { Modifier } "heap" [ "(" Expression ")" ] Name [ ":" Type ] [ "=" Expression ] .` — 在堆上取得实例存储的标识符种类（见 [5.5](#55-heap)）
- `FixedDefinition = { Modifier } "fixed" [ "@" ] Name [ GenericParameters ] [ ":" Type ] [ "=" Expression | Block ] .` — 初始化后不可被赋值的标识符种类（见 [5.4](#54-fixed)）
- `LinkableDefinition = { Modifier } "linkable" [ "(" StringLiteral ")" ] FunctionShorthand | { Modifier } "linkable" [ "(" StringLiteral ")" ] Name [ GenericParameters ] [ ":" Type ] [ "=" Expression | Block ] . …` — 保证标识符以外部符号暴露的标识符种类（见 [5.6](#56-linkable)）
- `ReturnStatement = "return" [ Expression ] .` — 通过传递返回值直接退出函数的语句（见 [5.11.2](#5112-return-语句)）
- `ThrowStatement = "throw" Expression .` — 在 `trap` 或 `catch` 中抛出一个值、交由 `trap` 拦截的本地控制流语句（见 [5.11.3](#5113-throw-语句)）
- `TrapStatement  = "trap" TrapBody { "catch" CatchCondition CatchBlock } [ "finally" FinallyBlock ] . …` — 拦截本块及内层 `trap` 中产生的抛出值的本地控制流语句（见 [5.11.4](#5114-trap-语句)）
- 程序处理错误和意外情况的方式（见 [5.11.1](#5111-异常处理)）
- `Statement = LocalDefinition …` — 代码块或文件体中可独立出现的语法单位（见 [5](#5-语句)）
- `ExpressionStatement = FunctionCallExpression .` — 只求值函数调用并丢弃返回值的语句（见 [5](#5-语句)）
- `FileBody = { Statement } .` — 文件顶层的语句序列（见 [5](#5-语句)）
- `ExtensionDeclaration = "extension" [ "for" ExtensionTarget ] Block .` — 为类型追加成员的声明（见 [3.9.3](#393-扩展)）
- `SetStatement          = AssignmentTarget "=" Expression . …` — 以目标值表达式覆盖赋值目标所占内存的语句（见 [5.8](#58-赋值语句)）
- `CompoundSetStatement  = AssignmentTarget ( "+=" | "-=" | "*=" | "/=" | "%=" | "&=" | "|=" | "^=" | "<<=" | ">>=" | "<<<=" | ">>>=" ) Expression .` — 复合赋值语句（见 [5.8](#58-赋值语句)）
- `BreakStatement = "break" [ Name ] .` — 退出控制流的逻辑控制流语句（见 [5.10.8](#5108-break-语句)）
- `ContinueStatement = "continue" [ Name ] .` — 继续控制流的逻辑控制流语句（见 [5.10.9](#5109-continue-语句)）
- `DoWhileStatement = "do" [ ":" Name ] Block "while" Expression .` — 根据条件选择是否继续执行代码块的逻辑控制流语句（见 [5.10.5](#5105-do-while-语句)）
- `ForStatement = "for" [ "&" ] Name [ ":" Type ] "in" TraverseTarget [ ":" Name ] Block | "for" TraverseTarget [ ":" Name ] Block . …` — 遍历数组、可迭代对象或区间并执行代码块的逻辑控制流语句（见 [5.10.6](#5106-for-语句)）
- `Range = Expression [ ".." Expression ] RangeOperator Expression .` — 区间（见 [5.10.6](#5106-for-语句)）
- `RangeOperator = ".." | "..!" | "..<" | "..<=" | "..>" | "..>=" .` — 区间运算符（见 [5.10.6](#5106-for-语句)）
- `IfStatement = "if" Expression Block { "else" "if" Expression Block } [ "else" Block ] .` — 根据条件选择执行代码块的逻辑控制流语句（见 [5.10.3](#5103-if-语句)）
- `MatchStatement  = "match" Expression [ ":" Name ] "{" { MatchCase } "}" . …` — 根据模式选择执行代码块的逻辑控制流语句（见 [5.10.7](#5107-match-语句)）
- `WhileStatement = "while" Expression [ ":" Name ] Block .` — 根据条件选择是否继续执行代码块的逻辑控制流语句（见 [5.10.4](#5104-while-语句)）
- 用于标识逻辑控制流语句的名称（见 [5.10.1](#5101-逻辑控制流名称)）

有关函数：

- `FunctionDeclarationSugar = { Modifier } "fixed" Name [ GenericParameters ] ":" FunctionTypeConstructor ( Block | VerbatimBlock ) .` — 在标识符定义中以 `Block` 替代 `= <函数字面量>` 的语法形式（见 [8.1](#81-函数定义语法糖)）
- `Parameters = Parameter { "," Parameter } . …` — 声明函数类型构造器的参数（见 [8.2](#82-参数)）
- `LiteralParameters = LiteralParameter { "," LiteralParameter } . …` — 声明函数字面量的参数（见 [8.2](#82-参数)）
- `ParameterList = Parameters [ "," "wildcard" ] | "wildcard" . …` — 函数类型构造器的参数列表（见 [3.7.3](#373-函数类型构造器)）
- `Parameter = [ "#" ] [ Name ":" ] [ "&" ] Type [ "=" Expression ] . …` — 函数类型构造器的参数（见 [8.2](#82-参数)）
- `LiteralParameter = [ "#" ] Name [ ":" [ "&" ] Type ] . …` — 函数字面量的参数（见 [8.2](#82-参数)）
- `Arguments = [ Argument { "," Argument } ] . …` — 调用方传入函数的值（见 [8.3](#83-实参)）
- 声明在扩展块内、`this` 参数的类型为扩展目标类型的函数常量，用于直接操作实例及实例成员（见 [8.4](#84-方法)）
- `CompileTimeExpression = "#" "(" Expression ")" .` — 在编译期求值表达式并把结果保留到运行时的形式（见 [11.3](#113-编译期表达式)）
- `CompileTimeBlock = "#" Block .` — 在编译期执行代码并把终产物保留到运行时的形式（见 [11.4](#114-编译期块)）
- 从同名函数常量中选取被调用者的编译期过程（见 [8.5](#85-重载决议)）

有关泛型：

- `MonomorphizationExpression = Identifier "<" [ Type ] { ";" [ Type ] } ">" .` — 对泛型标识符进行单态化的表达式（见 [10.2](#102-单态化表达式)）
- `GenericParameters = "<" GenericParameterDefinition { ";" GenericParameterDefinition } ">" . …` — 声明中的类型占位符，用于编译期单态化（见 [10.1](#101-泛型参数)）

有关修饰符：

- `AbstractModifier = "abstract" .` — 用于声明抽象类的修饰符（见 [9.9](#99-abstract-修饰符)）
- `AlignedModifier = "aligned" "(" IntegerLiteral ")" .` — 用于声明结构体默认对齐、结构体字段对齐或共同体成员偏移的带参修饰符（见 [9.13](#913-alignedn-修饰符)）
- `ImplicitableModifier = "implicitable" .` — 用于声明类型转换可隐式进行的修饰符（见 [9.7](#97-implicitable-修饰符)）
- `ManualModifier = "manual" .` — 使堆分配单位不受 NOLAA 管辖的修饰符（见 [9.8](#98-manual-修饰符)）
- `OverrideModifier = "override" .` — 用于声明重写方法的修饰符（见 [9.12](#912-override-修饰符)）
- `PrivateAccessibilityModifier = "private" .` — 声明仅在声明所在作用域内可访问的基础可访问性修饰符（见 [9.3](#93-private-修饰符)）
- `ProtectedAccessibilityModifier = "protected" .` — 声明可被声明所在作用域及其派生作用域访问的基础可访问性修饰符（见 [9.4](#94-protected-修饰符)）
- `PublicAccessibilityModifier = "public" .` — 声明可被所有作用域访问的基础可访问性修饰符（见 [9.2](#92-public-修饰符)）
- `ReadonlyModifier = "readonly" .` — 用于声明只读成员的修饰符（见 [9.6](#96-readonly-修饰符)）
- `ScopedModifier = "scoped" "(" ScopedTarget { "," ScopedTarget } ")" . …` — 以具名目标裁剪可访问性的可访问性裁剪修饰符（见 [9.5](#95-scoped-修饰符)）
- `SealedModifier = "sealed" .` — 用于声明不可被继承的类或不可被重写的方法的修饰符（见 [9.10](#910-sealed-修饰符)）
- `StaticModifier = "static" .` — 用于声明与类型关联、但不与实例关联的成员的修饰符（见 [9.14](#914-static-修饰符)）
- `VirtualModifier = "virtual" .` — 用于声明可被重写的方法的修饰符（见 [9.11](#911-virtual-修饰符)）
- `Modifier = AccessibilityModifier …` — 位于标识符种类之前、用于限定标识符行为的语法形式（见 [9](#9-修饰符)）
- `AccessibilityModifier = BaseAccessibilityModifier | ScopedModifier . …` — 限定标识符可被哪些作用域访问的修饰符（见 [9.1](#91-可访问性修饰符)）

有关模块：

- `UseStatement = "use" Identifier { "," Identifier } . …` — 将目标标识符的成员引入当前作用域的语句（见 [5.7](#57-use-语句)）
- `ModuleDeclaration = { Modifier } "module" Name Block .` — 组织标识符的命名空间（见 [7](#7-模块)）

有关内存模型：

- 分析堆分配单位生命周期的编译期工具（见 [12.2](#122-nolaa)）
- 为实例取得内存空间的行为（见 [12.1](#121-内存分配)）

有关程序：

- 程序启动时被调用的函数常量（见 [14.1](#141-主函数)）
- 程序从入口点开始、直到终止的执行次序（见 [14.2](#142-启动流程)）

有关 ABI：

- NovaSharp 编译产物在二进制层面互相调用的接口约定（见 [13.1](#131-novasharp-abi)）
- 把 NovaSharp 名字编码为二进制符号名的规则（见 [13.2](#132-名称修饰)）

有关设计基准：

- 对设计原则在 Built-In Library 的设计上的具体落地（见 [1.3](#13-built-in-library-设计原则)）
- NovaSharp 语法设计的根本宗旨（见 [1.1](#11-核心设计哲学)）
- 对核心设计哲学的具体落地（见 [1.2](#12-设计原则)）

有关附录：

- Built-In Library 强制附带的所有定义，包括运行时定义与编译器 API 定义（见 [15.2](#152-built-in-library-完整内容)）

### 15.2 Built-In Library 完整内容

**Built-In Library 完整内容**是 Built-In Library（见 [1.3](#13-built-in-library-设计原则)）强制附带的全部定义，包括运行时定义与编译器 API 定义。

```novasharp
/// @brief Represents the root class of the inheritance hierarchy.
abstract class Object
{

}

/// @brief Identifies the calling convention of a function type.
enumeration CallingConvention: integer(4)
{
    /// @brief The platform's default calling convention.
    PlatformDefault = 0;
    /// @brief The C declaration calling convention.
    CDeclaration = 1;
    /// @brief The standard calling convention.
    StandardCall = 2;
    /// @brief The fast calling convention.
    FastCall = 3;
    /// @brief The System V AMD64 calling convention.
    SystemVAMD64 = 4;
    /// @brief The this-call calling convention.
    ThisCall = 5;
    /// @brief The vector calling convention.
    VectorCall = 6;
    /// @brief The Microsoft x64 calling convention.
    MicrosoftX64 = 7;
}

/// @brief Identifies the target platform.
enumeration PlatformKind: cardinal(4)
{
    /// @brief The target is not one of the named platforms.
    Other = 0;
    /// @brief Microsoft Windows.
    Windows = 1;
    /// @brief Linux.
    Linux = 2;
    /// @brief Apple macOS.
    MacOS = 3;
    /// @brief FreeBSD.
    FreeBSD = 4;
}

/// @brief Identifies an instruction set architecture.
enumeration ArchitectureKind: cardinal(4)
{
    /// @brief The architecture is not one of the named architectures.
    Other = 0;
    /// @brief Intel x86.
    X86 = 1;
    /// @brief x86-64.
    X86_64 = 2;
    /// @brief 32-bit ARM.
    Arm = 3;
    /// @brief 64-bit ARM.
    Arm64 = 4;
    /// @brief 32-bit RISC-V.
    RiscV32 = 5;
    /// @brief 64-bit RISC-V.
    RiscV64 = 6;
}

/// @brief Exposes special values of 32-bit floating point.
extension for float(4)
{
    /// @brief IEEE 754 non-number.
    public linkable NotANumber: float(4);

    /// @brief IEEE 754 positive infinity.
    public linkable Infinite: float(4);
}

/// @brief Exposes special values of 64-bit floating point.
extension for float(8)
{
    /// @brief IEEE 754 non-number.
    public linkable NotANumber: float(8);

    /// @brief IEEE 754 positive infinity.
    public linkable Infinite: float(8);
}

/// @brief Serves as the base class of all exceptions.
class Exception
{
    /// @brief Holds the exception that caused this exception, if any.
    public InnerException: Optional<Exception>;

    extension
    {
        /// @brief Initializes the exception.
        /// @param this The exception being initialized.
        /// @param innerException The exception that caused this exception.
        public fixed Construct(this: Exception, innerException: Optional<Exception> = Optional<Exception>.Null)
        {
            this.InnerException = innerException;
        }
    }
}

/// @brief Thrown when a requested operation is not implemented.
class NotImplementedException: Exception
{
    extension
    {
        /// @brief Initializes the exception.
        /// @param this The exception being initialized.
        public fixed Construct(this: NotImplementedException)
        {
            base.Construct(innerException: Optional<Exception>.Null);
        }
    }
}

/// @brief Thrown when an integer division or remainder has a zero divisor.
class DivideByZeroException: Exception
{
    extension
    {
        /// @brief Initializes the exception.
        /// @param this The exception being initialized.
        /// @param innerException The exception that caused this exception.
        public fixed Construct(this: DivideByZeroException, innerException: Optional<Exception> = Optional<Exception>.Null)
        {
            base.Construct(innerException: innerException);
        }
    }
}

/// @brief Thrown when an index is outside the bounds of an array.
class OutOfRangeException: Exception
{
    extension
    {
        /// @brief Initializes the exception.
        /// @param this The exception being initialized.
        /// @param innerException The exception that caused this exception.
        public fixed Construct(this: OutOfRangeException, innerException: Optional<Exception> = Optional<Exception>.Null)
        {
            base.Construct(innerException: innerException);
        }
    }
}

/// @brief Thrown when memory allocation fails.
class AllocateFailedException: Exception
{
    extension
    {
        /// @brief Initializes the exception.
        /// @param this The exception being initialized.
        public fixed Construct(this: AllocateFailedException)
        {
            base.Construct(innerException: Optional<Exception>.Null);
        }
    }
}

/// @brief Thrown when memory deallocation fails.
class DeallocateFailedException: Exception
{
    extension
    {
        /// @brief Initializes the exception.
        /// @param this The exception being initialized.
        public fixed Construct(this: DeallocateFailedException)
        {
            base.Construct(innerException: Optional<Exception>.Null);
        }
    }
}

/// @brief Iterates over the elements of a sequence.
/// @tparam T The type of the elements.
interface IIterator<T>
{
    /// @brief Advances to the next element.
    /// @return The next element, or Null when there are no more elements.
    public fixed Next(this: IIterator<T>): Optional<T>;

    /// @brief Returns the iterator to its initial position.
    public fixed Reset(this: IIterator<T>);
}

/// @brief Exposes an iterator over the elements of a sequence.
/// @tparam T The type of the elements.
interface IIterable<T>
{
    /// @brief Gets an iterator over the elements.
    public accessor Iterator: IIterator<T> { get; }
}

/// @brief Provides heap allocation and deallocation.
interface IAllocator
{
    /// @brief Allocates memory.
    /// @param this The allocator.
    /// @param size The requested size in bytes.
    /// @param alignment The requested alignment in bytes.
    /// @return The allocated pointer, or an allocation failure.
    public fixed Allocate(this: IAllocator, size: cardinal(8), alignment: cardinal(8)): Result<pointer; AllocateFailedException>;

    /// @brief Deallocates memory.
    /// @param this The allocator.
    /// @param block The block to deallocate.
    /// @return An empty result, or a deallocation failure.
    public fixed Deallocate(this: IAllocator, block: pointer): Result<; DeallocateFailedException>;
}

/// @brief Represents an optional value.
variant Optional<T>
{
    Value: T;
    Null;

    extension
    {
        /// @brief Maps Optional to Result for ??.
        /// @param value The optional value.
        /// @return A Result whose Value carries T, or whose Alternative is empty.
        operator ??(value: Optional<T>): Result<T;>
        {
            match value
            {
                case is payload:Optional<T>.Value
                {
                    return Result<T;>.Value(payload);
                }
                case is Optional<T>.Null
                {
                    return Result<T;>.Alternative;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// @brief Represents a primary value or an alternative.
/// @tparam TValue The primary value type.
/// @tparam TAlternative The alternative type.
variant Result<TValue; TAlternative>
{
    Value: TValue;
    Alternative: TAlternative;

    extension
    {
        /// @brief Returns the result itself as the ?? decision.
        /// @param value The result to decide on.
        /// @return The same result.
        operator ??(value: Result<TValue; TAlternative>): Result<TValue; TAlternative>
        {
            return value;
        }
    }
}

#{
    /// @brief Exposes compile-time type queries.
    module Type
    {
        /// @brief Returns the size of T in bytes.
        /// @return A 64-bit unsigned value.
        public linkable SizeOf<T>(): cardinal(8);
    }

    /// @brief Exposes compile-time diagnostics.
    module Compiler
    {
        /// @brief Emits a compile-time note.
        /// @param message The message bytes.
        /// @param length The message byte length.
        public linkable LogNote(message[length: cardinal(4)]: cardinal(1));

        /// @brief Emits a compile-time warning.
        /// @param message The message bytes.
        /// @param length The message byte length.
        public linkable LogWarning(message[length: cardinal(4)]: cardinal(1));

        /// @brief Emits a compile-time error.
        /// @param message The message bytes.
        /// @param length The message byte length.
        public linkable LogError(message[length: cardinal(4)]: cardinal(1));

        /// @brief Asserts a compile-time condition.
        /// @param condition The condition to assert.
        public linkable Assert(condition: boolean);

        /// @brief Asserts a compile-time condition with a message.
        /// @param condition The condition to assert.
        /// @param message The message bytes.
        /// @param length The message byte length.
        public linkable Assert(condition: boolean, message[length: cardinal(4)]: cardinal(1));
    }

    /// @brief Identifies the execution world of the current code.
    enumeration EnvironmentKind: cardinal(4)
    {
        /// @brief The compiler host environment.
        Host = 0;
        /// @brief The target runtime environment.
        Target = 1;
    }

    /// @brief Describes a frozen execution environment.
    structure EnvironmentFacts
    {
        /// @brief The operating-system platform.
        public Platform: PlatformKind;

        /// @brief The instruction set architecture.
        public Architecture: ArchitectureKind;

        extension
        {
            /// @brief Tests a platform name.
            /// @param this The environment facts.
            /// @param name The platform name bytes.
            /// @param length The name byte length.
            /// @return True when the environment has this platform name.
            public linkable PlatformIs(this: EnvironmentFacts, name[length: cardinal(4)]: cardinal(1)): boolean;

            /// @brief Tests an architecture name.
            /// @param this The environment facts.
            /// @param name The architecture name bytes.
            /// @param length The name byte length.
            /// @return True when the environment has this architecture name.
            public linkable ArchitectureIs(this: EnvironmentFacts, name[length: cardinal(4)]: cardinal(1)): boolean;
        }
    }

    /// @brief Exposes compile-time environment queries.
    module Environment
    {
        /// @brief The execution world of the current code region.
        public linkable Kind: EnvironmentKind;

        /// @brief Facts of the current code region.
        public linkable Current: EnvironmentFacts;

        /// @brief Frozen host facts.
        public linkable Host: EnvironmentFacts;

        /// @brief Frozen target facts.
        public linkable Target: EnvironmentFacts;
    }

    /// @brief Exposes heap lifetime analysis controls.
    module NOLAA
    {
        /// @brief Stops automatic analysis of the pointed heap object.
        /// @param x The heap object pointer.
        public linkable Abandon(x: pointer);

        /// @brief Requires required to exist while requiredBy exists.
        /// @param required The heap object that must exist.
        /// @param requiredBy The heap object that needs required.
        public linkable Bind(required: pointer, requiredBy: pointer);

        /// @brief Assumes the next statement does not require x.
        /// @param x The heap object pointer.
        public linkable AssumeNoDemandNext(x: pointer);
    }
}
```
